Hooks run in Claude Code (terminal, IDE, desktop Code tab); in claude.ai chat only the skills load. The hooks make no network requests; review tools run by hand send text to OpenRouter. - **baton**: Stops your AI agent from re-reading your project every new session. A one-page handoff (HANDOFF.md), checked when it is saved, goes into the next session when you ask to continue, with a line that says how old it is. A concept map (CONCEPTS.md) ties each concept to its files and the commit it was last checked at, so only concepts whose files changed get a second look. AI가 새 대화마다 프로젝트를 처음부터 다시 읽지 않게 — 지난 대화는 인계서 한 장으로, 바뀐 곳은 개념 지도로. ([sulabo/baton](https://github.com/sulabo/baton) · 3 skills, 5 commands, 1 hook file)
